# AI Agent Index

This is the mandatory routing index for AI agents. Read this file after `AGENTS.md` and before planning or editing.

Purpose: make document access predictable as the project grows. Do not read every document by default. Select the smallest required set by task goal, then record the exact files in `Docs consulted`.

## Startup Algorithm

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A["Read AGENTS.md"] --> B["Read docs/agent-index.md"]
    B --> C["Classify user goal"]
    C --> D["Read required docs for that goal"]
    D --> E["Record Docs consulted"]
    E --> F{"Covered by active docs?"}
    F -->|"Yes"| G["Plan or implement through workflow"]
    F -->|"No / conflict"| H["Stop for docs update proposal or user-approved brief"]
```

## Index Groups

| Index | Group | Entry point | Use for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ROOT-00` | Human project map | [../README.md](../README.md) | Human-friendly project overview |
| `DOCS-00` | Docs map | [README.md](README.md) | Human + AI navigation across `docs/` |
| `AGENT-00` | AI routing | [agent-index.md](agent-index.md) | Required AI document selection |
| `FLOW-00` | AI workflow entry | [ai-development-workflow.md](ai-development-workflow.md) | Entry point ??88-line index that links sub-docs |
| `WF-00` | Workflow sub-docs | [ai-workflow/planning-contracts.md](ai-workflow/planning-contracts.md), [ai-workflow/context-and-packets.md](ai-workflow/context-and-packets.md), [ai-workflow/review-gates.md](ai-workflow/review-gates.md), [ai-workflow/fallback-and-recovery.md](ai-workflow/fallback-and-recovery.md) | Planning 路 ledger/packets 路 review gates 路 fallback matrix |
| `WF-10` | Ledger/report templates | [ai-workflow/README.md](ai-workflow/README.md) | Ledgers, reports, packets, plans (folder map + templates) |
| `HARNESS-00` | Harness and skills | [ai-workflow/harness-and-skills.md](ai-workflow/harness-and-skills.md) | TALKPIK, practical skills, GStack, Superpowers, host-specific skill names |
| `PRODUCT-00` | Product requirements | [prd.md](prd.md) | Product scope, users, value, business rules |
| `SPEC-00` | Implementation spec | [spec.md](spec.md) | Development baseline, behavior, validation, framework, backend, auth, AI boundary, deployment |
| `DEV-10` | Development details | [development/README.md](development/README.md) | Detailed technical specs selected through `SPEC-00` |
| `UI-00` | Design system | [ant-design/README.md](ant-design/README.md) | UI implementation, tokens, components, motion |
| `IA-00` | Information architecture | [ia.md](ia.md), [sitemap.md](sitemap.md) | Page hierarchy, routes, navigation |
| `IA-10` | Screen specs | [IA/README.md](IA/README.md) | Specific page descriptions and wireframes |
| `JOURNEY-00` | User journey | [flow/user-flow.md](flow/user-flow.md) | Step order, transitions, entry/exit states |
| `LEGACY-00` | Legacy observations | [ia-pages/README.md](ia-pages/README.md), [user-flow.md](user-flow.md) | Historical context only |

## Goal-To-Doc Routing

| User goal | Required docs | Conditional docs | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Product scope, user value, roles, business direction | `PRODUCT-00` | `SPEC-00`, `JOURNEY-00` | Do not invent new product direction. |
| Functional behavior, validation, data handling, acceptance criteria | `SPEC-00` | `PRODUCT-00`, `JOURNEY-00` | Use for implementation and tests. |
| Framework, package, dependency, backend, auth, deployment, env vars, deferred billing | `SPEC-00` | Matching `DEV-10` detail file | Start at `spec.md`; do not read all development details by default. |
| Navigation, routes, page hierarchy | `IA-00` | `JOURNEY-00`, `IA-10` | Use Target React Route Map, not legacy route notes. |
| User journey, screen order, transitions, entry/exit states | `JOURNEY-00` | `IA-10`, `SPEC-00` | `docs/user-flow.md` is legacy context only. |
| Visual UI, layout, components, tokens, motion | `UI-00` | `IA-10`, `JOURNEY-00` | Run design review before user-facing implementation. |
| Specific page or screen | `IA-10` matching page | `UI-00`, `JOURNEY-00`, `SPEC-00` | Read the matching `description.md` and inspect `wireframe.png`. |
| Admin ?旊┐ / 甓岉暅 / RBAC / audit log | `SPEC-00`, ?措嫻 admin `IA-10`(`docs/IA/{21-H-01,30-X-08,32-X-10}-*`), `DEV-10`(backend-auth) | `JOURNEY-00`, `UI-00`, `docs/ai-workflow/light-specs/phase-6-admin-library-hardening.md` | Admin?€ ?茧皹 ?毄?愳? 甓岉暅 氇嵏路UI 攵勱赴路QA ?滊倶毽槫臧€ ?る. | AI workflow, context, reports, fallback, multi-agent work | `FLOW-00`, `WF-00` | `HARNESS-00` | Required for workflow or harness changes. |
| Historical page composition | `LEGACY-00` | Active docs above | Reference only. Active docs win. |

## Development Detail Routing

Use this table only after reading [spec.md](spec.md).

| Development goal | Read |
| --- | --- |
| Frontend framework, package choice, runtime, UI library, forms, validation, charts, tests | [development/stack.md](development/stack.md) |
| Supabase, Auth, Postgres, RLS, Storage, server-only keys | [development/backend-auth.md](development/backend-auth.md) |
| Vercel, environments, deployment gates, environment variables, rollback, CI, preview links | [development/deployment.md](development/deployment.md) |
| Billing, subscription, paywall, payment provider, deferred scope | [development/deferred-scope.md](development/deferred-scope.md) |

## Active Vs Legacy Rule

Active docs govern implementation, QA, and review:

- [prd.md](prd.md)
- [spec.md](spec.md)
- [ant-design/README.md](ant-design/README.md)
- [sitemap.md](sitemap.md) Target React Route Map
- [ia.md](ia.md)
- [IA/README.md](IA/README.md) and matching `docs/IA/<page>/description.md`
- [flow/user-flow.md](flow/user-flow.md)

Legacy docs are reference only:

- [user-flow.md](user-flow.md)
- [ia-pages/README.md](ia-pages/README.md)
- Legacy HTML Route Map sections inside [sitemap.md](sitemap.md)

If active and legacy docs conflict, active docs win. If active docs conflict with the user request, stop and report the conflict.

## Ledger Requirement Index

Create or update a run ledger under `docs/ai-workflow/runs/YYYY/MM/DD/` when any of these apply:

| Trigger | Required |
| --- | --- |
| Non-trivial work | Yes |
| Implementation work | Yes |
| UI, route, flow, or integration changes | Yes |
| Net-new scope or doc conflict | Yes |
| Multi-agent work | Yes |
| Work likely to resume later | Yes |
| Tiny docs/config-only edit with no behavior change, no conflict, no multi-agent work, no resume risk | May skip with reason |

Use [ai-workflow/context-ledger-template.md](ai-workflow/context-ledger-template.md).

For consistency, classify the work as non-trivial when it changes workflow-governing files, automation, scripts, CI, routes, UI, auth, database, API, dependencies, deployment behavior, test strategy, or AI-service boundaries. Multi-file changes are non-trivial by default unless they are purely mechanical docs/config updates and the final report states the lightweight exception.

When a ledger is required, update it before final verification and include it in the dirty scope, commit trailers, PR body, or final report as applicable.

## Output Requirement

Every plan, handoff, or final report must include:

- `Docs consulted`: exact files read.
- `Extracted requirements`: concrete requirements taken from those docs.
- `Doc conflicts`: `none` or exact conflict references.
- `Untouched relevant docs`: docs that seemed related but were not read, with reason.
- `Context ledger`: path or allowed lightweight exception.

.\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:50:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. .\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:773:**5. RLS in exposed schemas.**
.\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:774:Enable RLS on every table in any exposed schema, which includes `public` by default. This is critical in Supabase because tables in exposed schemas can be reachable through the Data API when the `anon`/`authenticated` roles have access (see [Exposing a Table to the Data API](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/api/securing-your-api.md)). For private schemas, prefer RLS as defense in depth. After enabling RLS, create policies that match the actual access model rather than defaulting every table to the same `auth.uid()` pattern.
.\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:788:  - **Views bypass RLS by default.** In Postgres 15 and above, use `CREATE VIEW ... WITH (security_invoker = true)`. In older versions of Postgres, protect your views by revoking access from the `anon` and `authenticated` roles, or by putting them in an unexposed schema.
.\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:790:  - **Do not put `security definer` functions in an exposed schema.** Keep them in a private or otherwise unexposed schema.
.\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:841:**To make schema changes, use `execute_sql` (MCP) or `supabase db query` (CLI).** These run SQL directly on the database without creating migration history entries, so you can iterate freely and generate a clean migration when ready.
.\tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:843:Do NOT use `apply_migration` to change a local database schema ??it writes a migration history entry on every call, which means you can't iterate, and `supabase db diff` / `supabase db pull` will produce empty or conflicting diffs. If you use it, you'll be stuck with whatever SQL you passed on the first try.

.\tasks\codex-runs\stderr-3-fix-audit.txt:3279:tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:50:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. Adding a second audit row for system-initiated cleanup duplicates state and forces us to invent a synthetic actor or change schema. Operational visibility is preserved via `cron.job_run_details`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.

.\tasks\codex-runs\stderr-3-fix-audit.txt:3378:tasks\codex-prompt-email-confirmation-review-round2.md:38:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. Adding a second audit row for system-initiated cleanup duplicates state and forces us to invent a synthetic actor or change schema. Operational visibility is preserved via `cron.job_run_details`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.

.\tasks\codex-runs\stderr-3-fix-audit.txt:3466:tasks\codex-runs\stderr-2-playwright.txt:649:tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:50:  **Author's proposed resolution: drop the `admin_audit_logs` insert entirely.** Rationale: `pg_cron` already records every run in `cron.job_run_details` with `start_time`, `end_time`, `status`, `return_message`. The function will instead `RAISE LOG` the deleted count, which Supabase ships to logs.

.\tasks\codex-runs\stderr-2-playwright.txt:1982:tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:841:**To make schema changes, use `execute_sql` (MCP) or `supabase db query` (CLI).** These run SQL directly on the database without creating migration history entries, so you can iterate freely and generate a clean migration when ready.
.\tasks\codex-runs\stderr-2-playwright.txt:1983:tasks\codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round2-20260526.md:843:Do NOT use `apply_migration` to change a local database schema ??it writes a migration history entry on every call, which means you can't iterate, and `supabase db diff` / `supabase db pull` will produce empty or conflicting diffs. If you use it, you'll be stuck with whatever SQL you passed on the first try.

21:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
22:// Types
23:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
25:interface ThemeContextValue {
26:  theme: BuiltAppTheme;
27:  appearance: ThemeAppearance;
28:  setAppearance: (appearance: ThemeAppearance) => void;
29:}
31:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
32:// Context
33:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
35:const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextValue | null>(null);
37:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
38:// Provider
39:// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
41:interface ThemeProviderProps {
42:  children: ReactNode;
43:  /**
44:   * Appearance resolved server-side from cookie. Prevents first-paint flash.
45:   * Defaults to "light".
46:   *
47:   * NOTE (T2): After mount, the CLIENT owns theme state. Server re-renders
48:   * (router.refresh, etc.) do not override the user's selected appearance.
49:   * This is intentional â€” the user's toggle persists until they change it.
50:   */
51:  initialAppearance?: ThemeAppearance;
52:  /** Theme name. Only "default" exists today. Extend when adding presets. */
53:  themeName?: AppThemeName;
54:}
56:export function ThemeProvider({
57:  children,
58:  initialAppearance = defaultAppearance,
59:  themeName = defaultThemeName,
60:}: ThemeProviderProps) {
61:  const [appearance, setAppearanceState] =
62:    useState<ThemeAppearance>(initialAppearance);
64:  const theme = useMemo(
65:    () => getAppTheme(themeName, appearance),
66:    [themeName, appearance],
67:  );
69:  /**
70:   * Writes resolved --app-* CSS variables to <html> at runtime.
71:   * Called on mount and on every appearance change.
72:   */
73:  const applyVarsToDocument = useCallback(
74:    (nextAppearance: ThemeAppearance) => {
75:      // antd v6.x í˜¸í™˜ì„±: server-safe fallback. ë™ì  brand overrideê°€ ìƒê¸°ë©´
76:      // client ì¸¡ theme.useToken() hook ê²°ê³¼ë¡œ ì¶”ê°€ update í•„ìš”.
77:      const vars = getResolvedBridgeVarsByAppearance(nextAppearance);
78:      const el = document.documentElement;
79:      Object.entries(vars).forEach(([key, value]) => {
80:        el.style.setProperty(key, value);
81:      });
82:      el.style.setProperty("color-scheme", nextAppearance);
83:    },
84:    [],
85:  );
87:  const setAppearance = useCallback(
88:    (nextAppearance: ThemeAppearance) => {
89:      setAppearanceState(nextAppearance);
90:      applyVarsToDocument(nextAppearance);
91:      // Persist for next SSR render (1 year)
92:      document.cookie = `theme-appearance=${nextAppearance}; path=/; max-age=${60 * 60 * 24 * 365}; SameSite=Lax`;
93:    },
94:    [applyVarsToDocument],
95:  );
97:  // On mount: sync document vars with SSR-injected initialAppearance.
98:  // Handles edge cases where cached client vars may differ from the server.
99:  useEffect(() => {
100:    applyVarsToDocument(appearance);
101:    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
102:  }, []); // intentionally mount-only â€” appearance changes handled by setAppearance
104:  const value = useMemo(
105:    () => ({ theme, appearance, setAppearance }),
106:    [theme, appearance, setAppearance],
107:  );
109:  return (
110:    <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>
111:      {children}
112:    </ThemeContext.Provider>
113:  );
114:}

121:  const ctx = useContext(ThemeContext);
122:  if (!ctx) {
123:    throw new Error("useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider");
124:  }
125:  return ctx;
126:}

 succeeded in 892ms:
1:-- =====================================================================
2:-- TALKPIK AI 쨌 Phase 8-B 쨌 Unconfirmed user retention policy
3:--
4:-- Deletes auth.users with email_confirmed_at IS NULL and created_at older
5:-- than retention_days. Storage objects owned by victims are removed first
6:-- to avoid the "auth user owning storage objects cannot be deleted" failure.
7:-- public.profiles is removed via existing FK ON DELETE CASCADE (verified
8:-- invariant 2026-05-26).
9:--
10:-- Default retention 30 days. dry_run mode returns count without deleting.
11:-- max_batch caps per-call deletes to prevent runaway.
12:--
13:-- Function lives in private schema (already used by private.is_admin)
14:-- to avoid exposure through PostgREST. SECURITY DEFINER with locked
15:-- search_path so it can delete auth.users / storage.objects regardless of
16:-- caller role; revoked from public/anon/authenticated.
17:--
18:-- Spec: reports/email-confirmation-policy-research-20260526.html
19:-- Codex 3-round PASS consensus: tasks/codex-output-email-confirmation-review-round{1,2,3}-20260526.md
20:-- =====================================================================
22:create or replace function private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users(
23:  retention_days int default 30,
24:  dry_run boolean default false,
25:  max_batch int default 1000
26:)
27:returns int
28:language plpgsql security definer
29:set search_path = pg_catalog, public, auth, storage
30:as $$
31:declare
32:  victim_ids uuid[];
33:  deleted_count int;
34:begin
35:  if retention_days < 1 then
36:    raise exception 'retention_days must be >= 1 (got %)', retention_days;
37:  end if;
38:  if max_batch < 1 then
39:    raise exception 'max_batch must be >= 1 (got %)', max_batch;
40:  end if;
42:  select array_agg(id) into victim_ids
43:  from (
44:    select id from auth.users
45:    where email_confirmed_at is null
46:      and created_at < now() - make_interval(days => retention_days)
47:      and is_sso_user = false
48:    order by created_at
49:    limit max_batch
50:  ) sub;
52:  deleted_count := coalesce(array_length(victim_ids, 1), 0);
54:  if dry_run then
55:    raise log 'cleanup_unconfirmed_users dry_run: would delete % users older than % days',
56:      deleted_count, retention_days;
57:    return deleted_count;
58:  end if;
60:  if deleted_count > 0 then
61:    -- Storage objects first (Auth user delete conflict prevention; Codex Round 2 NF3)
62:    delete from storage.objects where owner = any(victim_ids);
64:    -- public.profiles auto-removed via FK ON DELETE CASCADE (verified invariant 2026-05-26)
65:    delete from auth.users where id = any(victim_ids);
67:    raise log 'cleanup_unconfirmed_users: deleted % users older than % days',
68:      deleted_count, retention_days;
69:  end if;
71:  return deleted_count;
72:end;
73:$$;
75:revoke all on function private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users(int, boolean, int)
76:  from public, anon, authenticated;
78:comment on function private.cleanup_unconfirmed_users(int, boolean, int) is
79:  'Delete unconfirmed auth.users older than N days (default 30) and their owned storage objects. '
80:  'SECURITY DEFINER with locked search_path. dry_run=true returns count without deleting. '
81:  'max_batch caps deletes per call. profiles row removed via FK ON DELETE CASCADE.';
속성을 설정할 수 없습니다. 속성 설정은 이 언어 모드의 핵심 유형에 대해서만 지원됩니다.

✅ **1. `layout.tsx` barrel 우회**
통과. `src/app/layout.tsx`는 더 이상 `@/theme` barrel을 import하지 않습니다.  
근거: `src/app/layout.tsx:9`는 `@/theme/tailwind-bridge`, `src/app/layout.tsx:10`은 `@/theme/types` 직접 import입니다.

✅ **2. server-safe 모듈만 평가**
통과. `tailwind-bridge.ts`는 런타임 import 없이 `import type`만 씁니다.  
근거: `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:17`, `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:19`. 실제 반환도 정적 값 기반입니다: `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:35-57`, `src/theme/tailwind-bridge.ts:63-67`.  
`types.ts`도 `antd`를 type-only로만 참조합니다: `src/theme/types.ts:1`.

✅ **3. client 쪽 barrel 사용 회귀 위험**
정상. `src/contexts/theme-context.tsx`는 `"use client"` 파일이고: `src/contexts/theme-context.tsx:1`, barrel import는 client 내부에서만 사용됩니다: `src/contexts/theme-context.tsx:13-19`.  
`src/app/providers.tsx`도 `"use client"`입니다: `src/app/providers.tsx:1`, `ThemeProvider`를 감싼 뒤 `ConfigProvider`에 넘깁니다: `src/app/providers.tsx:16-20`, `src/app/providers.tsx:57-59`.

✅ **4. 이전 8-10/10 항목 유지**
유지. 라운드 2의 남은 실패 사유는 `layout.tsx`의 `@/theme` barrel import 1건이었습니다: `tasks/codex-runs/output-4-fix-recheck.txt:33-45`. 현재 그 import는 직접 path로 바뀌었습니다: `src/app/layout.tsx:9-10`.
